<td className="mdescRight">Employs a topological query on a graph generated a-priori by <a className="el" href="/content/api/cs/classkinetica_1_1Kinetica/creategraph#a7f40c6fcc8881eb27a6676c905d46129">createGraph</a> and returns a list of adjacent edge(s) or node(s), also known as an adjacency list, depending on what's been provided to the endpoint; providing edges will return nodes and providing nodes will return edges. <br /></td>
</tr>
